 K.R.NAGARA TALUK, MYSORE DISTRICT – 570 001
 … RESPONDENTS
(BY SMT. RASHMI PATEL, HCGP FOR R1,R3,R4 & R6;
 SRI. G. V. SHASHIKUMAR, ADV. FOR R2)
THIS WRIT PETITION IS FILED UNDER ARTICLES 226 AND
227 OF THE CONSTITUTION OF INDIA PRAYING TO DECLARE
THAT SECTIONS 20(2) (A-IV) AND (A-V) OF THE KARNATAKA
CO-OPERATIVE SOCIETIES ACT, IS ULTRA VIRES THE
LEGISLATIVE COMPETENCE AND ARTICLE 243Z1 AND 243ZO OF
THE CONSTITUTION OF INDIA.
THIS WRIT PETITION COMING ON FOR ORDERS THIS DAY,
THE COURT MADE THE FOLLOWING:
ORDER
Heard Shri.Patil Sumanth Kumar S., learned advocate
for the petitioners, Smt. Rashmi Patel, learned HCGP for
respondent Nos.1, 3, 4 and 6 and Shri G.V.Shashi Kumar,
learned advocate for respondent No.2.
2. Shri.Patil Sumanth Kumar S., learned advocate
submits that elections have already been conducted.
Therefore, this petition is rendered infructuous and
accordingly prayed for its dismissal. He has filed a memo of
even date to the said effect. Memo be kept in record.

296

3. In the circumstance, petition is dismissed as having
become infructuous.
 No costs.
Sd/-
 JUDGE
MH/-
